公司簡介
CORPORATE PROFILE


我們是一家快速增長的全球化多品牌現製飲   We are a fast-growing global multi-brand freshly-made beverage
品公司，旗下擁有滬上阿姨、茶瀑布和滬咖   company with three major brands, namely Auntea Jenny (滬上阿姨),
三大品牌，通過多品牌矩陣，精準契合消費   Fallstea (茶瀑布) and Jenny x Coffee (滬咖). Through our multi-brand
者多元化的消費需求。我們以加盟業務為核   portfolio, we are able to precisely meet the diverse consumption
心，互利共贏的加盟體系是我們與加盟商之   needs of consumers. We operate a franchise-focused business
間長期穩定合作的基礎。在戰略佈局上，我   model. Our mutually beneficial franchise system is the foundation for
們聚焦下沉市場，按門店總數計算，我們在   our long-term and stable cooperation with franchisees. In terms of
中國下沉市場的中價現製茶飲店品牌中擁有   strategic presence, we focus on the lower-tier market. In terms of
強大市場地位。               the total number of stores, we hold a strong market position among
                      mid-priced freshly-made tea shop brands in the lower-tier market in
                      the PRC.

業務回顧                                       BUSINESS REVIEW
概況                                         Overview
我們是一家快速增長的全球化多品牌現製飲                        We are a fast-growing global multi-brand freshly-made beverage
品公司，旗下擁有滬上阿姨、茶瀑布和滬咖                        company with three major brands, namely Auntea Jenny (滬上阿姨 ),
三大品牌，通過多品牌矩陣，精準契合消費                        Fallstea (茶瀑布 ) and Jenny x Coffee (滬咖 ). Through our multi-brand
者多元化的消費需求。我們以加盟業務為核                        portfolio, we are able to precisely meet the diverse consumption
心，互利共贏的加盟體系是我們與加盟商之                        needs of consumers. We operate a franchise-focused business
間長期穩定合作的基礎。在戰略佈局上，我                        model. Our mutually beneficial franchise system is the foundation for
們聚焦下沉市場，按門店總數計算，我們在                        our long-term and stable cooperation with franchisees. In terms of
中國下沉市場的中價現製茶飲店品牌中擁有                        strategic presence, we focus on the lower-tier market. In terms of
強大市場地位。                                    the total number of stores, we hold a strong market position among
                                           mid-priced freshly-made tea shop brands in the lower-tier market in
                                           the PRC.

300多個城市，北至中國黑龍江省漠河，南                       regions and 22 provinces in China, extending northwards to Mohe,
至中國海南省三亞。2025年，我們積極拓                       Heilongjiang Province, China and southwards to Sanya, Hainan

      根據灼識諮詢，中國三線及以下城市的現製                    The freshly-made tea shop market in third and lower-tier cities
      茶飲店市場按商品交易總額計為2023年至                   in China is the largest and is expected to be the fastest-growing
      2028年期間最大且預期增長最快的細分市                   segment from 2023 to 2028 in terms of gross merchandise volume,
      場，未來增長潛力巨大。我們十分重視三線                    with significant potential for future growth according to CIC. We
      及以下城市，並於門店覆蓋率及供應鏈網絡                    place great emphasis on the third and lower-tier cities and have
      等方面具備優勢。截至2025年12月31日，                 advantages in terms of store coverage and supply chain network.
      我們三線及以下城市門店數佔比52.7%，同                  As of December 31, 2025, our stores in third and lower-tier cities
      比增長2.3個百分點。                            accounted for 52.7%, representing a 2.3 percentage points increase
                                             year on year.

      我們的加盟模式                                Our franchise model
      我們業務模式的核心為與我們志同道合、                     The core of our business model is cooperation with franchisees
      具有創業精神並樂於發展我們品牌及門店                     who are committed to our concept and highly motivated to grow
      網絡的加盟商合作。我們採用互惠互利的加                    our brand and store network. We have a mutually beneficial
      盟模式，初期投資較低，並提供全面的加盟                    franchise model which requires low initial investment and provides
      支持。根據我們的加盟安排，加盟商負責租                    comprehensive franchise support. Under our franchise arrangement,
      賃門店、管理門店經營及聘請員工。我們則                    our franchisees are responsible for leasing the stores, managing
      通過高度標準化及數字化的門店管理系統，                    the operation of the stores and hiring staff. Through our highly
      為加盟商提供全生命周期的支持及督導，包                    standardised and digitalised store management system, we provide
      括選址規劃及評估、開店支持、數字化經營                    comprehensive and lifecycle support and supervision including site
      支援、監督及培訓支持等，幫助加盟商解決                    selection planning and evaluation, store opening support, digitalized
      問題、改善經營，加之提供基於消費洞察及                    operation support, supervision and training support to franchisees,
      強大的全國供應鏈管理的優質及多樣化的產                    enabling franchisees to overcome challenges and improve
      品，不斷提升門店盈利能力。全面的加盟商                    operations. In addition, we continuously enhance store profitability
      支持平台、在下沉市場取得成功的往績及互                    by providing high-quality and diverse products based on consumer
      利共贏的加盟模式，為我們門店網絡的可持                    insights and a robust nationwide supply chain. The comprehensive
      續擴展提供支持。截至2025年12月31日，                 franchisee support platform, the proven track record of success in
      我們共有6,974名加盟商開設11,423家門店。              the lower-tier markets and the mutually beneficial franchise model
                                             all support the sustainable expansion of our store network. As
                                             of December 31, 2025, our franchise network comprised 6,974
                                             franchisees operating 11,423 stores.

2025年，我們新開設加盟門店數較2024年          In 2025, the number of our newly opened franchised stores
有明顯提升，特別是2025年下半年，基於            increased significantly compared with 2024. Particularly in the
門店業績的良好表現，本公司加速了門店擴             second half of 2025, leveraging the strong performance of store
張策略，我們的加盟門店數由2024年12月           operations, the Company accelerated its store expansion strategy,
31日的9,152家，增長至2025年12月31日的      and our franchised stores increased from 9,152 as at December 31,
11,423家。我們亦高度關注門店經營質量及          2024 to 11,423 as at December 31, 2025. We also placed great
渠道網絡運營效率，根據經營狀况對已有門             emphasis on the operating quality of our stores and the operational
店進行管理、幫扶及優化調整。結合門店擴             efficiency of our channel network, and managed, supported and
張策略，按照有進有退原則，主動優化網絡             optimised existing stores based on their operating conditions.
佈局，在門店規模擴張的同時，關閉的加盟             Adopting our store expansion strategy and upholding the principle
門店數相應有所增加。一方面是由於該部分             of selective expansion and closure, we proactively optimised our
加盟商無法以理想盈利經營門店或門店租賃             network presence. Concurrent with the expansion in store scale,
到期無法獲得替代地址等原因；另一方面，             the number of closed franchised stores also increased. On the
為進一步提升消費者體驗和門店經營質量，             one hand, this was due to factors such as these franchisees being
我們對門店佈局進行了主動優化調整，有序             unable to operate stores at a desirable level of profitability or being
退出部分加盟門店。未來公司仍將持續聚焦             unable to secure alternative locations upon expiration of lease.
門店經營質量提升和盈利能力優化，助力加             On the other hand, to further enhance consumer experience and
盟商穩健經營，提升服務水平。                  quality of store operations, we proactively optimised and adjusted
                                our store presence, and phased out certain franchised stores
                                in an orderly manner. In the future, the Company will continually
                                focus on improving the quality of store operations and increasing
                                the profitability of stores, assisting franchisees in achieving stable
                                operations and enhancing the service level.

      產品研發                                 Product development
      我們建立了全面的產品開發系統，致力於開                  We have established an integrated product development system
      發獨特的產品以及改良現有產品，以超越顧                  and are committed to developing distinctive products and refining
      客的期望並吸引新顧客。我們的產品應新鮮                  our existing products to exceed our customers’ expectations and
      時令食材、流行趨勢及消費者不斷變化的口                  to attract new customers. Our products are evolved based on fresh
      味而演變。除楊枝甘露及厚芋泥波波奶茶等                  seasonal ingredients, prevailing trends and the changing tastes of
      全年均大受歡迎的主打產品外，我們亦提供                  consumers. In addition to staple items such as Mango Pomelo Sago

非國際財務報告準則計量                   Non-IFRS measures
為補充按照國際財務報告準則會計準則呈            To supplement our consolidated financial statements that are
列的綜合財務報表，我們亦使用並非國際            presented in accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards, we also
財務報告準則會計準則規定或按其呈列的經           use adjusted profit for the year (a non-IFRS measure) and adjusted
調整年內溢利（非國際財務報告準則計量）及          net profit margin (a non-IFRS measure), as additional financial
經調整淨利率（非國際財務報告準則計量）作          measures, which are not required by, or presented in accordance
為附加財務計量指標。我們認為此等非國際           with IFRS Accounting Standards. We believe that these non-IFRS
財務報告準則計量通過消除若干項目的潛在           measures facilitate comparisons of operating performance from
影響，有助於就不同期間的經營業績進行對           period to period by eliminating potential impact of certain items. We
比。我們認為，此等計量指標為投資者及其           believe that these measures provide useful information to investors
他人士提供有用信息，使其以與有助於管理           and others in understanding and evaluating our consolidated financial
層相同的方式了解並評估我們的綜合財務報           statements in the same manner as they help our management.
表。然而，我們所呈列的經調整年內溢利            However, our presentation of adjusted profit for the year (a non-
（非國際財務報告準則計量）及經調整淨利率          IFRS measure) and adjusted net profit margin (a non-IFRS measure)

司所呈列類似項目計量指標相比。此等非國           companies. The use of these non-IFRS measures has limitations as
際財務報告準則計量指標用作分析工具存在           an analytical tool, and you should not consider them in isolation from,
局限性， 閣下不應將其視為獨立於或可替           or as substitute for analysis of, our consolidated financial statements
代我們根據國際財務報告準則會計準則所呈           or financial condition as reported under IFRS Accounting Standards.
報綜合財務報表或財務狀況的分析。我們將           We define adjusted profit for the year (a non-IFRS measure) as profit
經調整年內溢利（非國際財務報告準則計量）          for the year adjusted by adding the share-based payment expenses
定義為就以股份為基礎的付款開支及上市開           and listing expenses. We define adjusted net profit margin (a non-

       主要業務                                 PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES
       我們是一家快速增長的全球化多品牌現製飲                  We are a fast-growing global multi-brand freshly-made beverage
       品公司，旗下擁有滬上阿姨、茶瀑布和滬咖                  company with three major brands, namely Auntea Jenny (滬上阿姨),
       三大品牌，通過多品牌矩陣，精準契合消費                  Fallstea (茶瀑布) and Jenny x Coffee (滬咖). Through our multi-brand
       者多元化的消費需求。我們以加盟業務為核                  portfolio, we are able to precisely meet the diverse consumption
       心，互利共贏的加盟體系是我們與加盟商之                  needs of consumers. We operate a franchise-focused business
       間長期穩定合作的基礎。在戰略佈局上，我                  model. Our mutually beneficial franchise system is the foundation for
       們聚焦下沉市場，按門店總數計算，我們在                  our long-term and stable cooperation with franchisees. In terms of
       中國下沉市場的中價現製茶飲店品牌中擁有                  strategic presence, we focus on the lower-tier market. In terms of
       強大市場地位。                              the total number of stores, we hold a strong market position among
                                            mid-priced freshly-made tea shop brands in the lower-tier market in
                                            the PRC.

遵守企業管治守則                              COMPLIANCE WITH CORPORATE GOVERNANCE
                                      CODE
本集團致力於維持高水平的企業管治以保障                   The Group is committed to maintaining high standards of corporate
股東利益、提升企業價值及問責性。本集團                   governance to safeguard the interests of Shareholders and to
已採納企業管治守則作為其本身的企業管治                   enhance corporate value and accountability. The Group has adopted
守則。據董事所知，除以下偏離企業管治守                   the Corporate Governance Code as its own code of corporate
則第二部分的守則條文第C.2.1條情況外，                 governance. During the period from the Listing Date and up to the
本公司自上市日期起直至本報告日期已全面                   date of this report, to the best knowledge of the directors, except for
遵守企業管治守則載列的所有適用守則條                    the deviation from code provision C.2.1 of part 2 of the Corporate
文。                                    Governance Code below, the Company has fully complied with all
                                      applicable code provisions in the Corporate Governance Code.

根據企業管治守則第二部分的守則條文第                    Pursuant to code provision C.2.1 of part 2 of the Corporate
C.2.1 條 ， 主 席 與 行 政 總 裁 的 角 色 應 有 區   Governance Code, the roles of chairman and chief executive officer
分，並不應由同一人擔任。主席與行政總裁                   should be separate and should not be performed by the same
之間的職責分工應清楚界定並以書面列載。                   individual. The division of responsibilities between the chairman and
於報告期間內，本公司董事會主席及最高行                   chief executive officer should be clearly established and set out in
政人員由單衛鈞先生一人擔任，其主要負責                   writing. During the Reporting Period, the roles of chairman of the

色，單先生作為我們的行政總裁，對我們的                   implementation of our Group. Our Board believes that, in view of

有效及迅速的整體戰略規劃。此外，董事會                   Board also believes that vesting the roles of both chairperson
合共七名董事中有三名為獨立非執行董事，                   and chief executive officer in the same person has the benefit of

       董事會多元化                               BOARD DIVERSITY
       本公司已採納董事會多元化政策，並規定了                  The Company has adopted a Board diversity policy, which sets out
       實現董事會多元化的方式。本公司認識到並                  the means for achieving diversity on the Board of Directors. The
       接受多元化董事會的裨益，並將提升董事會                  Company recognizes and embraces the benefits of a diversified
       層面的多元化視為令本公司保持可持續發展                  Board, and considers enhancing diversity at the Board level as
       及達成戰略目標的重要因素。                        an important factor for the Company to maintain sustainable
                                            development and achieve its strategic goals.

       根據董事會多元化政策，對所有董事的任命                  Pursuant to the Board diversity policy, all appointments of Directors
       均以用人唯才為原則，於選擇董事會成員候                  are made on a merit basis, and in selecting candidates for the Board,
       選人時，在符合適當條件的前提下，充分考                  the benefits of diversity among Board members are fully considered,
       慮董事會成員多元化的裨益。本公司將於選                  subject to appropriate conditions being met. The Company will
       擇董事候選人時進行多個方面的考量，包括                  consider a number of aspects when selecting candidates for
       但不限於性別、年齡、文化及教育背景、專                  Directors, including but not limited to gender, age, cultural and
       業經驗、技能、知識及服務任期。是否獲任                  educational background, professional experience, skills, knowledge
       命最終取決於篩選出的候選人能為董事會創                  and length of service. Whether an appointment is made ultimately
       造的價值和貢獻。                             depends on the value and contribution the selected candidate can
                                            bring to the Board.

       我們的目標是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存                  Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether
       在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取                  the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from
       得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見的核數師                  material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an
       報告。我們的報告僅向 閣下（作為一個整                  auditor’s report that includes our opinion. Our report is made solely
       體）作出，除此之外，本報告不可用作其他                  to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do not assume
       用途。我們不會就本報告的內容對任何其他                  responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person for the
       人士負上或承擔任何責任。                         contents of this report.

       合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證根據                  Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a
       香港審計準則進行的審計總能發現重大錯誤                  guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will
       陳述。錯誤陳述可因欺詐或錯誤產生，倘合                  always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements
       理預期它們單獨或滙總起來可能影響使用者                  can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,
       根據綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則被                  individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected
       視為重大錯誤陳述。                            to influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of
                                            these consolidated financial statements.

       作為根據香港審計準則進行審計的一部分，                  As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise
       我們在整個審計過程中運用專業判斷，保持                  professional judgement and maintain professional scepticism
       專業懷疑態度。我們亦：                          throughout the audit. We also:

       •   識別及評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜                 •    Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the
           合財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風                       consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or
           險，設計及執行審計程序以應對該等                      error, design and perform audit procedures responsive to
           風險，以及獲取充足適當的審計憑                       those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and
           證，作為我們意見的基礎。未能發現                      appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of
           因欺詐而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險                      not detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is
           高於未能發現因錯誤而導致的重大錯                      higher than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve
           誤陳述的風險，此乃由於欺詐可能涉                      collusion, forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or

           述，或凌駕於內部控制之上。

             該等財務報表乃根據國際財務報告準                   These financial statements have been prepared in accordance
             則會計準則編製，其包括經國際會計                   with IFRS Accounting Standards, which comprise all standards
             準則理事會批准的所有準則及詮釋、                   and interpretations approved by the International Accounting
             經國際會計準則委員會批准的國際會                   Standards Board and International Accounting Standards and
             計準則及常設詮釋委員會詮釋以及香                   Standing Interpretations Committee interpretations approved
             港公司條例的披露要求。其乃按歷史                   by the International Accounting Standards Committee and
             成本慣例編製，惟按公允價值計入損                   the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies
             益的金融投資或資產及按公允價值計                   Ordinance. They have been prepared under the historical
             入其他全面收入的金融投資按公允價                   cost convention, except for financial investments or assets at
             值計量除外。除另有指明者外，該等                   fair value through profit or loss and financial investments at
             財務報表以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，                 fair value through other comprehensive income which have
             而所有數值均四捨五入至最接近的千                   been measured at fair value. These financial statements are
             位數。                                presented in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded to
                                                the nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.

             綜合基準                               Basis of consolidation
             綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公                   The consolidated financial statements include the financial
             司（統稱「本集團」）截至2025年12月31             statements of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively
             日止年度的財務報表。附屬公司為本                   referred to as the “Group”) for the year ended 31 December
             公司直接或間接控制的實體（包括結構                  2025. A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity),
             性實體）。當本集團對參與被投資公司                  directly or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control
             的浮動回報承擔風險或享有權利，以                   is achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to
             及能透過對被投資公司的權力（即現時                  variable returns from its involvement with the investee and has
             賦予本集團指示被投資公司相關活動                   the ability to affect those returns through its power over the
             的現有權利）影響該等回報時，即取得                  investee (i.e. existing rights that give the Group the current
             控制權。                               ability to direct the relevant activities of the investee).

             一般而言，擁有過半數投票權會被假                   Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting
             定為取得控制權。若本公司擁有被投                   rights results in control. When the Company has less than a
             資公司的投票權或類似權利少於半                    majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group
             數，則本集團於評估是否對被投資公                   considers all relevant facts and circumstances in assessing
             司擁有權力時會考慮所有相關事實及                   whether it has power over an investee, including:
             情況，包括：

             (a)   與被投資公司其他投票持有人的               (a)   the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders
                   合約安排；                              of the investee;

  製。附屬公司的業績自本公司取得控         consistent accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries

  算直至上述控制權終止當日止。           control, and continue to be consolidated until the date that
                           such control ceases.

  即使會導致非控股權益結餘出現虧          Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive
  絀，損益及其他全面收入各個組成部         income are attributed to the owners of the parent of the Group
  分歸屬於本集團母公司擁有人及非控         and to the non-controlling interests, even if this results in the
  股權益。所有與本集團成員公司之間         non-controlling interests having a deficit balance. All intra-
  交易有關之集團內公司間資產及負          group assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses and cash
  債、權益、收入、開支及現金流量已         flows relating to transactions between members of the Group
  於綜合時全數抵銷。                are eliminated in full on consolidation.

   倘有事實及情況顯示上述三項控制權        The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if
   要素有一項或以上出現變動，本集團        facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one
   會重新評估其是否對被投資公司擁有        or more of the three elements of control described above. A
   控制權。附屬公司擁有權權益的變動        change in the ownership interest of a subsidiary, without a loss
  （並無失去控制權）
          作為權益交易入賬。        of control, is accounted for as an equity transaction.

  倘本集團失去對一間附屬公司的控制         If the Group loses control over a subsidiary, it derecognises
  權，則會終止確認相關資產（包括商         the related assets (including goodwill), liabilities, any non-
  譽）、負債、任何非控股權益及匯兌波        controlling interest and the exchange fluctuation reserve; and
  動儲備；並確認所保留任何投資的公         recognises the fair value of any investment retained and any
  允價值及損益賬中任何因此產生的盈         resulting surplus or deficit in profit or loss. The Group’s share
  餘或虧絀。先前於其他全面收入內確         of components previously recognised in other comprehensive
  認的本集團應佔部分，按倘本集團直         income is reclassified to profit or loss or retained profits, as
  接出售相關資產或負債所要求的相同         appropriate, on the same basis as would be required if the

       2.2 會計政策及披露變動                        2.2 CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND
                                                DISCLOSURES
            本集團於本年度財務報表首次採納國                    The Group has adopted amendments to IAS 21 Lack of
            際會計準則第21號的修訂「缺乏可兌換                  Exchangeability for the first time for the current year’s financial
            性 」。本集團並無提早採納任何其他已                  statements. The Group has not early adopted any other
            頒佈但尚未生效的準則或修訂。                      standard or amendment that has been issued but is not yet
                                                effective.

             國際會計準則第21號的修訂訂明，當                  Amendments to IAS 21 specify how an entity shall assess
             缺乏可兌換性時，實體應如何評估一                   whether a currency is exchangeable into another currency and
             種貨幣是否可兌換為另一種貨幣，以                   how it shall estimate a spot exchange rate at a measurement
             及如何估計其於計量日的即期匯率。                   date when exchangeability is lacking. The amendments
             該等修訂要求披露使財務報表的使用                   require disclosures of information that enable users of
             者能夠了解貨幣不可兌換的影響的資                   financial statements to understand the impact of a currency
             料。由於本集團交易的貨幣以及海外                   not being exchangeable. As the currencies that the Group
             附屬公司用以換算為本集團呈列貨幣                   had transacted in and the functional currencies of overseas
             的功能貨幣均可兌換，該等修訂對本                   subsidiaries for translation into the Group’s presentation
             集團財務報表並無任何影響。                      currency were exchangeable, the amendments did not have
                                                any impact on the Group’s financial statements.

             此外，國際會計準則理事會已頒佈對                   In addition, the IASB has issued amendments to Illustrative
             國際財務報告準則第7號、國際財務報                  Examples on IFRS 7, IFRS 18, IAS 1, IAS8, IAS 36 and IAS 37
             告準則第18號、國際會計準則第1號、                 Disclosures about Uncertainties in the Financial Statements,
             國際會計準則第8號、國際會計準則                   which added illustrative examples in the corresponding IFRS
             第36號及國際會計準則第37號中關於                 Accounting Standards. These examples reflect existing
             財務報表不確定性披露的說明性示例                   requirements in the corresponding IFRS Accounting Standards
             的修訂，其於相應的國際財務報告會                   to report the effects of uncertainties in the financial statements
             計準則中增加了說明性示例。該等示                   using climate related examples. Therefore, the amendments do
             例反映相應國際財務報告會計準則中                   not have an effective date or transitional provisions.
             的現有規定，即使用氣候相關示例在
             財務報表中報告不確定性的影響。因
             此，該等修訂並無生效日期或過渡性
             條文。

            小計。其亦要求於單一附註中披露由                     requires disclosures about management-defined performance

2.4 重大會計政策資料             2.4 M A T E R I A L A C C O U N T I N G P O L I C Y
                             INFORMATION
  公允價值計量                     Fair value measurement
  本集團以各報告期末時的公允價值計           The Group measures certain financial assets at fair value at the
  量若干金融資產。公允價值為在市場           end of each of the reporting periods. Fair value is the price that
  參與者於計量日期進行之有序交易中           would be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability
  就出售資產所收取或就轉讓負債所支           in an orderly transaction between market participants at the
  付的價格。公允價值計量乃根據出售           measurement date. The fair value measurement is based on
  資產或轉讓負債的交易於資產或負債           the presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer
  的主要市場或（在沒有主要市場情況           the liability takes place either in the principal market for the asset
  下）資產或負債的最具優勢市場進行的          or liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in the most
  假設而作出。主要或最具優勢市場須           advantageous market for the asset or liability. The principal
  為本集團可進入的市場。資產或負債           or the most advantageous market must be accessible by the
  的公允價值乃按市場參與者（假設其會          Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability is measured using
  以最佳經濟利益行事）於資產或負債定          the assumptions that market participants would use when
  價時所用的假設計量。                 pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market participants
                             act in their economic best interest.

  非金融資產的公允價值計量須考慮市           A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into
  場參與者透過按其最高及最佳用途使           account a market participant’s ability to generate economic
  用該資產或將其出售予將按其最高及           benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by
  最佳用途使用該資產的另一市場參與           selling it to another market participant that would use the asset
  者而產生經濟效益的能力。               in its highest and best use.

  本集團使用適用於有關情況，且有足           The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in
  夠數據可計量公允價值，能最大限度           the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to
  使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減少           measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable
  使用不可觀察輸入數據的估值技術。           inputs and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.

              集團管理有關資產的業務模式。除不                   characteristics and the Group’s business model for managing

  初步確認及計量（續）               Initial recognition and measurement (continued)
  本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其         The Group’s business model for managing financial assets
  管理金融資產以產生現金流的方法。         refers to how it manages its financial assets in order to
  業務模式決定現金流乃來自收取合約         generate cash flows. The business model determines whether
  現金流、出售金融資產或同時來自兩         cash flows will result from collecting contractual cash flows,
  者。按攤銷成本進行分類及計量的金         selling the financial assets, or both. Financial assets classified
  融資產乃於以持有金融資產以收取合         and measured at amortised cost are held within a business
  約現金流為目的的業務模式中持有，         model with the objective to hold financial assets in order to

                                                  INFORMATION (continued)
              金融資產減值                             Impairment of financial assets
              本集團就所有並非以按公允價值計入                   The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses
              損益的方式持有的債務工具確認預期                   (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through
              信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。預                 profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the
              期信貸虧損乃以根據合約到期支付的                   contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract
              合約現金流與本集團預期收取的所有                   and all the cash flows that the Group expects to receive,
              現金流之間的差額，按概約原有實際                   discounted at an approximation of the original effective interest
              利率折現計算得出。預期現金流將包                   rate. The expected cash flows will include cash flows from the
              括出售所持抵押品或其他信貸增級（為                  sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements that are
              合約條款的不可分割部份）的現金流。                  integral to the contractual terms.

             一般方法                                General approach
             預期信貸虧損分兩階段確認。自初步                    ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for
             確認以來信貸風險並無大幅上升的信                    which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk
             貸風險敞口而言，會就未來12個月可                   since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses
             能出現的違約事件所產生信貸虧損（12                  that result from default events that are possible within the next
             個月預期信貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損                   12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for
             撥備。至於自初步確認以來信貸風險                    which there has been a significant increase in credit risk since
             急升的信貸風險敞口而言，則須就其                    initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses
             餘下風險年期的信貸虧損（不論出現違                   expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective
             約的時間）確認虧損撥備（整個存續期                   of the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).
             預期信貸虧損）。

            於各報告日期，本集團會評估金融工                     At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the
            具的信貸風險自初步確認以來是否已                     credit risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly
            大幅上升。於作出評估時，本集團會                     since initial recognition. When making the assessment,
            將報告日期金融工具的違約風險與首                     the Group compares the risk of a default occurring on the
            次確認日期金融工具的違約風險進行                     financial instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a
            比較，並會考慮毋須過多成本或努力                     default occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of
            即可取得的合理及有理據支持的資料                     initial recognition and considers reasonable and supportable
            （包括歷史及前瞻性資料）。倘合約付                    information that is available without undue cost or effort,
            款逾期超過30日，本集團會視其信貸                    including historical and forward-looking information. The Group
            風險已大幅上升。                             considers that there has been a significant increase in credit
                                                 risk when contractual payments are more than 30 days past
                                                 due.

              倘合約付款逾期90日，本集團會視該                  The Group considers a financial asset in default when
              項金融資產違約。然而，在若干情況                   contractual payments are 90 days past due. However, in
              下，倘有內部或外部資料顯示，在不                   certain cases, the Group may also consider a financial asset
              計及本集團所持有的任何信貸增級的                   to be in default when internal or external information indicates
              情況下，本集團可能無法全數收回未                   that the Group is unlikely to receive the outstanding contractual
              償還合約金額時，本集團亦會視該項                   amounts in full before taking into account any credit
              金融資產違約。倘合理預期未能收回                   enhancements held by the Group. A financial asset is written
              合約現金流，則會將該項金融資產撇                   off when there is no reasonable expectation of recovering the
              銷。                                 contractual cash flows.

  簡單方法                      Simplified approach
  就不包含重大融資成分的應收貿易賬          For trade receivables and contract assets that do not contain a

  權宜方法而不會就重大融資成分的影          practical expedient of not adjusting the effect of a significant
  響調整而言，本集團採用簡化法進行          financing component, the Group applies the simplified
  預期信用損失計量。根據簡化法，本          approach in calculating ECLs. Under the simplified approach,
  集團不會追蹤信貸風險的變化，而是          the Group does not track changes in credit risk, but instead
  於各報告日期按全期預期信用損失確          recognises a loss allowance based on lifetime ECLs at each

  終止確認金融負債                 Derecognition of financial liabilities
  金融負債於負債的責任獲解除、註銷         A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under
  或屆滿時終止確認。                the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.

  當一項現有金融負債被同一放款人按         When an existing financial liability is replaced by another from
  極為不同的條款所發行的另一項金融         the same lender on substantially different terms, or the terms of
  負債所取代時，或現有負債的條款作         an existing liability are substantially modified, such an exchange
  出重大修改時，有關交換或修訂被視         or modification is treated as a derecognition of the original
  為終止確認原有負債及確認一項新負         liability and a recognition of a new liability, and the difference
  債，相關賬面值間之差額於損益表中         between the respective carrying amounts is recognised in the
  確認。                      statement of profit or loss.

  抵銷金融工具                   Offsetting of financial instruments
  倘現時有可依法強制執行權利將已確         Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the net
  認金額抵銷，且有意按淨額基準結算         amount is reported in the statement of financial position if there
  有關金額或同時將資產變現及負債結         is a currently enforceable legal right to offset the recognised
  清時，金融資產與金融負債互相抵銷。        amounts and there is an intention to settle on a net basis, or to
                           realise the assets and settle the liabilities simultaneously.

  存貨                       Inventories
  存貨以成本與可變現淨值兩者之較低         Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable
  者列賬。成本按每月加權平均成本基         value. Cost is determined on the monthly weighted average
  準釐定。可變現淨值乃根據估計售價         cost basis. Net realisable value is based on estimated selling
  減達致完成及出售所產生的估計成本         prices less any estimated costs to be incurred to completion
  計算。                      and disposal.

  現金及現金等價物                 Cash and cash equivalents
  財務狀況表內的現金及現金等價物包         Cash and cash equivalents in the statement of financial
  括現金及銀行結餘及銀行活期存款，         position comprise cash on hand and at banks, and short-term
  以及可隨時轉換為已知金額現金的短         highly liquid deposits with a maturity of generally within three
  期高流動性存款（一般於三個月內到         months that are readily convertible into known amounts of
  期），其價值轉動風險不大，並為就應        cash, subject to an insignificant risk of changes in value and

      租賃－估計增量借款利率                          Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing rate
      本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的                     The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in
      利率，因此，其使用增量借款利率                      a lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate
     （「增量借款利率」）計量租賃負債。增                    (“IBR”) to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest
      量借款利率為本集團於類似經濟環境                     that the Group would have to pay to borrow over a similar
      中為取得與使用權資產價值相近的資                     term, and with a similar security, the funds necessary to obtain
      產，而以類似抵押品與類似期間借入                     an asset of a similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar
      所需資金應支付的利率。因此，增量                     economic environment. The IBR therefore reflects what the
      借 款 利 率 反 映 本 集 團「 應 支 付 」的 利        Group “would have to pay”, which requires estimation when
      率，當無可觀察的利率時（如就並無訂                    no observable rates are available (such as for subsidiaries that
      立融資交易的附屬公司而言）或當須對                    do not enter into financing transactions) or when it needs to
      利率進行調整以反映租賃的條款及條                     be adjusted to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease
      件時，則須作出利率估計（例如，當                     (for example, when leases are not in the subsidiary’s functional
      租賃並非以附屬公司的功能貨幣計值                     currency). The Group estimates the IBR using observable
      時）。本集團使用可觀察輸入數據（如                    inputs (such as market interest rates) when available and is
      市場利率）  （如有）估計增量借款利率，                 required to make certain entity-specific estimates (such as the
      並須作出若干實體特定的估計（如附屬                    subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).
      公司的獨立信貸評級）。

     非金融資產減值                               Impairment of non-financial assets
     本集團於2025年及2024年12月31日評                The Group assessed whether there were any indicators of
     估所有非金融資產（包括使用權資產）                     impairment for all non-financial assets (including the right-
     有否任何減值跡象。其他非金融資產                      of-use assets) at 31 December 2025 and 2024. Other non-
     在有跡象顯示賬面值可能無法收回時                      financial assets are tested for impairment when there are
     進行減值測試。當資產或現金產生單                      indicators that the carrying amounts may not be recoverable.
     位的賬面值超出其可收回金額（為公允                     An impairment exists when the carrying value of an asset or a
     價值減出售成本及使用價值兩者中的                      cash-generating unit exceeds its recoverable amount, which
     較高者）時，即存在減值。公允價值減                     is the higher of its fair value less costs of disposal and its value
     出售成本乃按類似資產按公平原則進                      in use. The calculation of the fair value less costs of disposal
     行的交易中，來自具約束力銷售交易                      is based on available data from binding sales transactions in
     的所得數據，或可觀察市價扣除出售                      an arm’s length transaction of similar assets or observable

        11. 所得稅開支                              11. INCOME TAX EXPENSE
              本集團須就本集團成員公司所處及經                       The Group is subject to income tax on an entity basis on profits
              營所在司法權區產生或源自有關司法                       arising in or derived from the jurisdictions in which members of
              權區的溢利，按實體基準繳納所得稅。                      the Group are domiciled and operate.

              根據《中國企業所得稅法》（「《企業所得                    Under the Law of the PRC on Enterprise Income Tax (the “EIT
              稅法》」）及《企業所得稅法》實施條例，                    Law”) and Implementation Regulation of the EIT Law, the EIT
              除下文所載的稅項寬免外，本集團中                       rate of the Group’s PRC subsidiaries is 25% unless subject to
              國附屬公司的企業所得稅稅率為25%。                     tax exemption set out below.

              本集團其中一家中國附屬公司從事「西                      One of the Group’s PRC subsidiaries is engaged in businesses
              部地區鼓勵類產業」，可享15%的優惠                     in the “Encouraged Industries in the Western Region” and
              企業所得稅稅率。                               eligible for the preferential EIT rate of 15%.

              本集團其中一家中國附屬公司在「橫琴                      One of the Group’s PRC subsidiaries is engaged in businesses
              廣東－澳門深度合作區」開展業務，可                      in the “Guangdong-Macao In-Depth Cooperation Zone in
              享15%的優惠企業所得稅稅率。                        Hengqin” and eligible for the preferential EIT rate of 15%.

              本集團兩家中國附屬公司被認定為「高                      Two of the Group’s PRC subsidiaries are accredited as “High
              新技術企業」，於截至2025年12月31                   and New Technology Enterprises” and were entitled to a
              日止年度可享15%的優惠所得稅稅率。                     preferential income tax rate of 15% during the year ended 31
                                                     December 2025.

              本集團若干中國附屬公司符合小微企                       Certain of the Group’s PRC subsidiaries are qualified as small
              業資格，截至2025年及2024年12月31                 and micro enterprises and were entitled to a preferential EIT
              日止年度，應課稅收入不足人民幣3百                      rate of 5% for the taxable income below RMB3 million during

              集團獲准通過獲授權進行外匯業務的                       and Payment of Foreign Exchange Regulations, the Group

                                                     banks authorised to conduct foreign exchange business.

              動利率計息。銀行存款的存款期介乎                       bank deposit rates. The bank deposits are made for varying
              六個月至三年不等，視乎本集團的現                       periods of between six months and three years depending on
              金管理而定。銀行結餘及存款存放於                       the cash management of the Group. The bank balances and
              信譽良好且近期並無違約記錄的銀                        deposits are deposited with creditworthy banks with no recent
              行。現金及現金等價物的賬面值與彼                       history of default. The carrying amounts of the cash and bank

   本集團於本年度及過往年度的保留溢            The amounts of the Group’s retained profits and the

                               of the Group.

   (a)   資本儲備                  (a)   Capital reserve
         資本儲備指本集團的注資及以股              The capital reserve represents capital contribution of
         份為基礎的付款儲備。資本儲備              the Group and share-based payment reserves. Details
         變動詳情載於財務報表的綜合權              of the movement in capital reserve are set out in the
         益變動表。                       consolidated statement of changes in equity of the
                                     financial statements

   (b)   法定盈餘儲備                (b)   Statutory surplus reserve
         根據《中國公司法》，本集團若干             In accordance with the PRC Company Law, certain
         屬內資企業的附屬公司須將根據              subsidiaries of the Group which are domestic enterprises
         有關中國會計準則釐定的除稅後              are required to allocate 10% of their profit after tax,
         溢利的10%轉撥至各自的法定盈             as determined in accordance with the relevant PRC
         餘儲備，直至儲備達到各自註冊              accounting standards, to their respective statutory surplus
         資本的50%。除《中國公司法》所            reserves until the reserves reach 50% of their respective
         載的若干限制外，法定盈餘儲備              registered capital. Subject to certain restrictions set out
         的一部份可轉換為股本，惟於作              in the PRC Company Law, part of the statutory surplus
         出資本化後的餘額不得少於註冊              reserves may be converted to share capital, provided
         資本的25%。                     that the remaining balance after the capitalisation is not
                                     less than 25% of the registered capital.

   (c)   按公允價值計入其他全面收入的        (c)   Fair value reserve of financial investments at fair
         金融資產的公允價值儲備                 value through other comprehensive income
         按公允價值計入其他全面收入的              The fair value reserve of financial investments at fair value
         金融資產的公允價值儲備包括指              through other comprehensive income compromises the
         定為按公允價值計入其他全面收              cumulative losses of financial investment designed as fair
         入的股權投資的累計虧損，詳情              value through other comprehensive income, as further
         於附註17進一步解釋。                 explained in note 17.

  (1) 第1級的金融工具                (1) Financial instruments in level 1
      在活躍市場買賣的金融工具的公                The fair values of financial instruments traded in active
      允價值根據各報告日期的市場報                markets are based on quoted market prices at each of
      價計算。倘所報價格為即時及可                the reporting dates. A market is regarded as active if
      定期從交易所、交易商、經紀、                quoted prices are readily and regularly available from an
      行業組織、定價服務或監管機關                exchange, dealer, broker, industry group, pricing service,
      取得，且該等價格為按公平原                 or regulatory agency, and those prices represent actual
      則進行的真實及定期發生的市場                and regularly occurring market transactions on an arm’s
      交易，則有關市場被視為活躍市                length basis. The quoted market price used for financial
      場。本集團持有的金融資產採用                assets held by the Group is the current bid price. These
      的市場報價為當前的買入價。該                instruments are included in level 1.
      等工具列入第1級。

  (2)   第2級的金融工具              (2)   Financial instruments in level 2
        第2級適用於第1級所包括報價以             Level 2 applies to assets for which there are inputs other
        外的其他可直接（即價格）或間接             than quoted market prices included within level 1 that are
        （即自價格衍生）自資產觀察所得             observable for the asset, either directly (that is, as prices)
        的輸入數據。分類列作第2級的              or indirectly (that is, derived from prices). The financial
        金融資產為理財產品及結構性存              assets classified as level 2 are wealth management
        款。理財產品的公允價值根據發              products and structured deposits. The fair value of
        行銀行所提供的估值結果釐定。              wealth management products is determined based
        發行銀行根據市場利率及有關相              on the valuation results provided by the issuing bank.
        關資產的到期期限釐定理財產品              The issuing bank determined the value of the wealth
        的價值。結構性存款的公允價值              management products based on market interest rates
        乃根據收入法釐定。                   and maturity terms of the relevant underlying assets. The
                                    fair value of structured deposits is determined based on
                                    the income approach.

38. 財務風險管理目標及政策           38. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES
                              AND POLICIES
  本集團的主要金融工具包括現金、銀           The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise cash bank
  行結餘及向具聲譽銀行購買的理財產           balances and wealth management products and structured
  品及結構性存款。該等金融工具的主           deposits purchased from reputable commercial banks. The
  要目的是支持本集團的營運。本集團           main purpose of these financial instruments is to support the
  有多款其他金融資產及負債，如自營           Group’s operations. The Group has various other financial
  運直接產生的貿易應付款項。              assets and liabilities such as trade payables which arise directly
                             from its operations.

  本集團金融工具所帶來的主要風險為           The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments
  信貸風險及流動性風險。由於本集團           are credit risk and liquidity risk. As the Group’s exposure to
  將對該等風險敞口保持在最低水平，           these risks is kept to a minimum, the Group has not used any
  本集團並無使用任何衍生工具及其他           derivatives and other instruments for hedging purposes. The
  工具作對沖之用。本集團並無持有或           Group does not hold or issue derivative financial instruments for
  發行衍生金融工具作買賣用途。董事           trading purposes. The board of directors reviews and agrees
  會檢討並協定管理該等風險的政策，           policies for managing these risks and they are summarised
  並概述如下。                     below.

  信貸風險                       Credit risk
  本集團只與知名且信貸良好的第三方           The Group trades only with recognised and creditworthy third
  進行交易。此外，本集團一直監察應           parties. In addition, receivable balances are monitored on an
  收款項結餘，因此本集團面臨的壞賬           ongoing basis and the Group’s exposure to bad debts is not
  風險敞口並不重大。                  significant.

  年末分階段最大風險敞口                Maximum exposure year-end staging

  質素及所面對的最大信貸風險敞口（除          which is mainly based on past due information unless other
  非有其他毋須過多成本或工作即可取           information is available without undue cost or effort, and
  得的資料，否則主要以逾期資料作根           year-end staging classification as at 31 December 2025 and
  據），以及於年末時按階段作出的分           2024. The amounts presented are gross carrying amounts for

              流動性風險                                                  Liquidity risk
              本集團使用循環流動性計劃工具監察                                       The Group monitors its risk to a shortage of funds using a
              資金短缺的風險。該工具考慮其金融                                       recurring liquidity planning tool. This tool considers the maturity
              工具及金融資產的到期日以及來自營                                       of both its financial instruments and financial assets and
              運的預計現金流量。                                              projected cash flows from operations.

              本集團的流動性主要取決於其營運維                                       The liquidity of the Group is primarily dependent on its ability
              持足夠的現金流入以履行其到期債務                                       to maintain adequate cash inflows from operations to meet
              責任的能力，以及其獲取外部融資以                                       its debt obligations as they fall due, and its ability to obtain
              應付已承諾的未來資本開支的能力。                                       external financing to meet its committed future capital
                                                                     expenditure.

              產的風險特徵管理其資本架構及作出                          adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions

              團可調整向股東派發的股息、向股東                          maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust

              任何外部施加的資本要求所限制。於                          shareholders or issue new shares. The Group is not subject to
              年內，概無對管理資本的目的、政策                          any externally imposed capital requirements. No changes were
              或程序作出變更。                                  made in the objectives, policies or processes for managing
